# Product inventory

Gamezilla is a local-first multiplayer platform: people open a profile, host spaces and play chess with friends on phones, tablets and computers. Source: [README.md#L1](../../README.md#L1). This atlas is a review draft, not an approved replacement for existing specifications. Original Gherkin and Quint files remain unchanged.

The context map is retained exactly. It does not name accountable people or teams; “owner” below identifies the context that must nominate one. The conformance manifest separately lists Chessboard, while the map does not; Whole product appears in a glossary but not the map. Those disagreements are in [spec-conflicts.md](spec-conflicts.md).

## Bounded contexts

| Context | Owner | Aggregate projections | Publishes | Consumes (relationship interpretation) | Atlas |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Identity | Identity owner (not named in specs) | account-privacy, account-sign-in, username-changes | SignedIn, SignedOut, UsernameChanged, AccountDeletionConfirmed | None documented | [account-privacy](contexts/profile/flows/account-privacy/machine.yaml) |
| Billing | Billing owner (not named in specs) | payment-provider-switch, membership-lifecycle, membership-machine | PaidAccessStarted, PaidAccessEnded | SignedIn, SignedOut, AccountDeletionConfirmed | [membership-and-checkout](contexts/billing/flows/membership-and-checkout/machine.yaml) |
| Spaces | Spaces owner (not named in specs) | space-recovery, space-names, space-hosting | SpaceOpened, PersonJoined, PersonLeft, SpaceEnded | SignedIn, SignedOut, UsernameChanged, AccountDeletionConfirmed, PaidAccessStarted, PaidAccessEnded, BecameFriends, FriendshipEnded | [hosting-and-joining](contexts/spaces/flows/hosting-and-joining/machine.yaml) |
| Friends | Friends owner (not named in specs) | friendship, notification-machine | BecameFriends, FriendshipEnded | SignedIn, SignedOut, UsernameChanged, AccountDeletionConfirmed, SpaceOpened, PersonJoined, PersonLeft, SpaceEnded | [published-event-reactions](contexts/friends/flows/published-event-reactions/machine.yaml) |
| Chess | Chess owner (not named in specs) | multiplayer, multiplayer-spaces, custom-game-control, custom-game-play, puzzle-attempt, puzzle-loading, custom-game, puzzle-feed, puzzle-sound, puzzle-result-recovery, puzzle-link, board-premoves, board-annotations, board-lifecycle, board-promotion, board-independence, board-keyboard, board-options | None published | SignedIn, SignedOut, UsernameChanged, AccountDeletionConfirmed, PaidAccessStarted, PaidAccessEnded, SpaceOpened, PersonJoined, PersonLeft, SpaceEnded, BecameFriends, FriendshipEnded, MatchFormed | [puzzle-solving-and-recovery](contexts/chess/flows/puzzle-solving-and-recovery/machine.yaml) |
| Auth | Auth owner (not named in specs) | private-apple-email, outside-accounts | None published | SignedIn, SignedOut, AccountDeletionConfirmed | [outside-account-sign-in](contexts/auth/flows/outside-account-sign-in/machine.yaml) |
| Room | Room owner (not named in specs) | tally, match-recovery, tally-recovery, ownership, match-clock | None published | None documented | [tally](contexts/room/flows/tally/machine.yaml) |
| Matchmaking | Matchmaking owner (not named in specs) | matching | MatchFormed | None documented | [matching](contexts/matchmaking/lifecycles/matching/machine.yaml) |
| Sync engine | Sync engine owner (not named in specs) | note-conflicts, note-refusal-recovery, note-recovery, note-replication | None published | None documented | [note-replication](contexts/sync-engine/lifecycles/note-replication/machine.yaml) |
| Database indexing | Database indexing owner (not named in specs) | index-retirement, index-interruption | None published | None documented | [index-interruption](contexts/db-indexes/lifecycles/index-interruption/machine.yaml) |
| App | App owner (not named in specs) | app-platform | None published | SignedIn, SignedOut, UsernameChanged, AccountDeletionConfirmed, SpaceOpened, PersonJoined, PersonLeft, SpaceEnded, BecameFriends, FriendshipEnded | [app-shell](contexts/runtime/flows/app-shell/machine.yaml) |
| Developer tools | Developer tools owner (not named in specs) | No context-local aggregate model | None published | SpaceOpened, PersonJoined, PersonLeft, SpaceEnded, BecameFriends, FriendshipEnded | [report-explorer](contexts/developer-tools/flows/report-explorer/machine.yaml) |
| Product feedback | Product feedback owner (not named in specs) | feedback draft (code-derived), reproduction recording (code-derived) | None published | None documented | [feedback-draft](contexts/feedback/lifecycles/feedback-draft/machine.yaml) |
| Journeys | Journeys owner (not named in specs) | No context-local aggregate model | None published | SignedIn, SignedOut, UsernameChanged, PaidAccessStarted, PaidAccessEnded, SpaceOpened, PersonJoined, PersonLeft, SpaceEnded, BecameFriends, FriendshipEnded | [critical-journeys](contexts/journeys/flows/critical-journeys/machine.yaml) |

Consumer event subsets are interpretations of the published context relationships, not verified subscriber registrations. Each is listed for owner review in [interactions.md](interactions.md). No invented domain event names fill missing published contracts.

## Routes and UI hosts

This inventory records every literal route declaration and every source file named screen, modal or sheet found in application source. Dynamic routes and unnamed embedded overlays still need an engineering review; the scan is not a proof of render reachability. Shell account routes belong to Auth mechanics; the account identity and privacy outcome belongs to Identity. Notifications and search follow Friends ownership from the map. Framework UI examples are not new product contexts.

## Quint modules and lifecycle inventory

| Model | Declared public actions | Disposition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [specs/auth/outside-accounts.qnt](../../specs/auth/outside-accounts.qnt) | init, signIn, confirmMatchingAccount, keepSeparate, connectSignInMethod, removeSignInMethod, signOut, signOutEverywhere, stealSignIn, loseConnection, connectionReturns, troubleStarts, troubleEnds, stopUsingSignInWithApple, deleteAppleAccount, chanceToConfirmLapses, signInsLapse, step | Mapped: [outside-accounts](contexts/auth/lifecycles/outside-accounts/machine.yaml) |

## Gherkin features and scenarios

All 231 features and 1,520 scenario declarations are classified in [gherkin-mapping.md](gherkin-mapping.md). Scenario Outlines expand to 1,636 concrete cases. Each mapped example preserves its parsed Background, Given facts, When events, Then checks and example-row identifier. Calculation and service-only examples are excluded from screen modeling with an explicit reason.

## Roles, plans and switches

| Fact or switch | Effect | Atlas / disposition | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Guest | Immediate profile; one hosted live space; 20 rated puzzle starts per UTC day; account-only choices denied. | [hosting-and-joining](contexts/spaces/flows/hosting-and-joining/machine.yaml); [puzzle-solving-and-recovery](contexts/chess/flows/puzzle-solving-and-recovery/machine.yaml) | README.md:61; specs/conformance.yaml; libs/permissions-shared/src/permission-policy.ts |
| Free account | Account-only social, privacy, matchmaking and feed choices; one hosted live space; unlimited rated puzzle starts. | Owning context flow branches; permission decisions remain the authority. | libs/permissions-shared/src/permission-policy.ts |
| Plus member | Unlimited hosted spaces; paid access follows membership, grace and last confirmation. | Billing, Spaces and Chess charts. | specs/spaces/hosting-limit.dmn; specs/chess/daily-puzzle-limit.dmn |
| Space host / member | Host selects game, renames and ends; membership does not confer host authority. | Spaces and Custom Game examples. | specs/spaces/space-roles.dmn; libs/permissions-shared/src/space-roles.ts |
| Puzzle manager | Only permitted team members can add puzzles. | puzzle-ingest and puzzle-loading charts. | specs/chess/puzzle-management-access.dmn |
| Billing enabled / configured provider | No plans, checkout or portal without the configured provider. | membership-and-checkout provider unavailable branch. | libs/permissions-shared/src/permission-policy.ts |
| Apple deployment availability | Google everywhere; Apple needs all bindings and is refused in production. | Auth provider-availability examples; review exact deployment seed. | README.md; specs/profile/apple-sign-in.feature |
| Permission policy version / history | All grants, restrictions, denials and unlock choices follow synced policy. | No separate Permissions boundary invented; owned outcomes appear in their original contexts. | libs/permissions-shared/src/permission-policy.ts |
| Puzzle auto-advance off / immediately / after one second | A live solve may advance; restored solves and failures never auto-advance. | puzzle-attempt-resume and feed charts; KI-001. | libs/chess-puzzle-client/src/use-puzzle-auto-advance.ts |
| Theme, sound, reduced motion, input and board options | Change visible appearance, sound or available controls. | runtime/settings, board option and sound charts. | specs/runtime/settings.feature; specs/chess-board/ |
| Harness modes, local-stack switches and test hooks | Choose evidence adapters and test observability. | Excluded from person-facing behavior; execution notes bind real/local worlds. | AGENTS.md; apps/server/src/product-server.ts; README.md |

The root conformance manifest’s complete capability list and DMN files remain the permission source inventory; they are linked from the owning context examples, not redefined here.

## Third-party and runtime integrations

| Integration | Incoming / outgoing facts | Sandbox availability | Atlas / exclusion | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Google OAuth | Provider sign-in outcome, verified address, cancellation or refusal. Outgoing authorization request. | Mockingbird OAuth follows the real protocol. | Auth sign-in, matching accounts and provider trouble. | specs/auth/; pkgs/auth-inhouse-server/src/ |
| Apple sign-in | Authorization, private address, stop-using and account-deleted facts; outgoing authorization. | Mock-only outside production. | Auth and Identity Apple examples. | README.md; specs/auth/private-apple-email.qnt |
| Stripe | Checkout, portal, subscription updates, declined payments, refunds and cancellation. | Mockingbird Stripe in harness and local stack. | Billing lifecycle and checkout; provider event names remain internal until adapted to published BillingEvent. | pkgs/billing-providers-server/src/billing-providers.ts; pkgs/billing-stripe-server/src/ |
| Paddle | Configured membership-provider checkout, portal and membership callbacks. | Provider support must be confirmed; a Stripe sandbox does not prove Paddle conformance. | Billing provider-switch charts and open question. | pkgs/billing-providers-server/src/billing-providers.ts; pkgs/billing-paddle-shared/ |
| MQTT: AWS IoT / EMQX / Aedes | Invalidation and room delivery; never new published product events. | In-process or sidecar transport in harness. | Live-update and offline examples; wire shapes excluded. | README.md:94; libs/infra-shared/tofu/ |
| Cloudflare Workers and Durable Objects | Application requests, room schedules and one-minute outbox sweep. | Worker handler runs behind injected local ports. | Runtime / Room visible recovery; infrastructure-only states excluded. | apps/product/wrangler.toml; apps/server/src/product-server.ts |
| Object storage: S3 / R2 | Uploads, private exports and immutable report parts. | Mockingbird S3 and in-memory ObjectStore ports. | Identity exports, feedback evidence, developer reports; bucket internals excluded. | README.md; specs/runtime/stored-files.feature |
| Speech / language model ports | Optional assistant speech and language output; core board observation stays grounded. | InMemoryTtsProvider / InMemoryLlmProvider locally; production is null in current composition. | Assistant examples retained as intended behavior; unavailable speech variant requires review. | apps/server/src/product-server.ts:308; specs/chess/puzzle-assistant.feature |
